<div class="col-sm order-1">
      <h2 class="chapter_title">CHAPTER 122A. TEACHERS AND OTHER EDUCATORS</h2>
      
      <div class="sr" id="stat.122A.001"><b><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.001">122A.001</a></b> MS 2006 [Renumbered 15.001]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.01">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.01 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <p>For purposes of this chapter, except for section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.41">122A.41</a>, the Teacher Tenure Act for cities of the first class, the words defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120A.05">120A.05</a> have the same meanings.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.01.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/71/#laws.6.1.0">Ex1959 c 71 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.8.101.0">1998 c 397 art 8 s 101</a>; art 11 s 3</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.04">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.04 LICENSE REQUIRED.</h1>
        <p>Pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120A.22#stat.120A.22.10">120A.22, subdivision 10</a>, a teacher must hold a field license or a permission aligned to the content area and scope of the teacher's assignment to provide instruction in a public school, including a charter school.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.04.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.5.5.0">2023 c 55 art 5 s 5</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">PROFESSIONAL EDUCATOR LICENSING AND STANDARDS BOARD</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.05">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.05 PROFESSIONAL PRACTICES; INTENT.</h1>
        <p>The purpose of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.05">122A.05</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.09">122A.09</a> is to develop standards of ethical conduct for the guidance and improvement of the teaching profession and to provide measures through which the observance of standards by the members of the profession may be promoted and enforced.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.05.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/25/#laws.0.1.0">Ex1967 c 25 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/749/#laws.0.7.0">1973 c 749 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.8.56.0">1998 c 397 art 8 s 56</a>,101; art 11 s 3</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.06">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.06 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.06.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purpose of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.04">122A.04</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.093">122A.093 </a>and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.15">122A.15</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.33">122A.33</a>, the terms defined in this section have the meanings given them, unless another meaning is clearly indicated.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.06.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Teacher.</span></h2>
          <p>"Teacher" means a classroom teacher or other similar professional employee required by law to hold a license from the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.06.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Board.</span></h2>
          <p>"Board" means the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.06.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.3.12.0">2023 c 55 art 3 s 12</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.06.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Field, licensure area, or subject area.</span></h2>
          <p>A "field," "licensure area," or "subject area" means the content area in which a teacher may become licensed to teach.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.06.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Shortage area.</span></h2>
          <p>"Shortage area" means:</p>
          <p>(1) licensure fields and economic development regions reported by the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board as experiencing a teacher shortage; and</p>
          <p>(2) economic development regions where the aggregate percentage of Indigenous teachers and teachers of color in the region is lower than the aggregate percentage of kindergarten through grade 12 Indigenous students and students of color in that region. Only individuals who close the gap between these percentages qualify as filling a shortage by this definition.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.06.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Teacher preparation program or unit.</span></h2>
          <p>"Teacher preparation program" means a program approved by the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board for the purpose of preparing individuals for a specific teacher licensure field in Minnesota.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.06.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Teacher preparation program provider.</span></h2>
          <p>"Teacher preparation program provider" or "unit" means an entity that has primary responsibility for overseeing and delivering a teacher preparation program. Teacher preparation program providers include institutes of higher education, school districts, charter schools, or nonprofit corporations organized under chapter 317A.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.06.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">District.</span></h2>
          <p>"District" means a school district or charter school.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.06.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Transfer pathway.</span></h2>
          <p>"Transfer pathway" means an established pathway to licensure between a two-year college or Tribal college, and a board-approved teacher preparation provider.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.06.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/25/#laws.0.2.0">Ex1967 c 25 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/749/#laws.0.8.0">1973 c 749 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/271/#laws.0.6.0">1975 c 271 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.20.0">1976 c 222 s 20</a>,21,27,208; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/345/#laws.0.8.0">1980 c 345 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/609/#laws.6.26.0">1980 c 609 art 6 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/12/#laws.8.20.0">1Sp1985 c 12 art 8 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.8.101.0">1998 c 397 art 8 s 101</a>; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/13/#laws.0.2.0">1Sp2001 c 13 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/5/#laws.2.33.0">1Sp2005 c 5 art 2 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/96/#laws.2.14.0">2009 c 96 art 2 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/272/#laws.1.11.0">2014 c 272 art 1 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.12.1.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 12 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.5.6.0">2023 c 55 art 5 s 6</a>-13</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.07">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.07 PROFESSIONAL EDUCATOR LICENSING AND STANDARDS BOARD MEMBERSHIP.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.07.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Appointment of members.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board consists of 13 members appointed by the governor, with the advice and consent of the senate. Membership terms, removal of members, the filling of membership vacancies, and fiscal year and reporting requirements are as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.07">214.07</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.09">214.09</a>. No member may be reappointed for more than one additional term.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.07.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Eligibility; board composition.</span></h2>
          <p> Each appointee, other than a public nominee, must be selected on the basis of professional experience and knowledge of teacher education, accreditation, and licensure. The board must be composed of:</p>
          <p>(1) seven teachers who are currently teaching in a Minnesota school, have at least five years of teaching experience, and are not serving in a position requiring an administrative license, pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.14">122A.14</a>. The seven teachers must include the following:</p>
          <p>(i) one teacher in a charter school;</p>
          <p>(ii) two teachers from a school located in the seven-county metropolitan area, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/473.121">473.121</a>, subdivision 2;</p>
          <p>(iii) two teachers from a school located outside the seven-county metropolitan area;</p>
          <p>(iv) one teacher from a related service category licensed by the board; and</p>
          <p>(v) one special education teacher;</p>
          <p>(2) two teachers currently teaching in a board-approved teacher preparation program;</p>
          <p>(3) one superintendent, alternating each term between a superintendent from a school district in the seven-county metropolitan area, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/473.121#stat.473.121.2">473.121, subdivision 2</a>, and a superintendent from a school district outside the metropolitan area;</p>
          <p>(4) one educator who oversees a special education program and who works closely with a cooperative unit under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/123A.24#stat.123A.24.2">123A.24, subdivision 2</a>;</p>
          <p>(5) one principal, alternating each term between an elementary and a secondary school principal; and</p>
          <p>(6) one member of the public that may be a current or former school board member.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.07.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.5.67.0">2023 c 55 art 5 s 67</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.07.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Vacant position.</span></h2>
          <p>With the exception of a teacher who retires from teaching during the course of completing a board term, the position of a member who leaves Minnesota or whose employment status changes to a category different from that from which appointed is deemed vacant.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.07.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote"> Terms, compensation; removal; vacancies.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The review and processing of complaints; the setting of fees; the selection and duties of an executive director to serve the board; and other provisions relating to board operations not provided in this chapter are as provided in chapter 214. Membership terms, except as provided in subdivision 2a, removal of members, the filling of membership vacancies, and fiscal year and reporting requirements are as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.07">214.07</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.09">214.09</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) Board members must receive a stipend of up to $4,800 annually, prorated monthly, during each year of service on the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.07.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Administration.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The executive director of the board shall be the chief administrative officer for the board but shall not be a member of the board. The executive director shall maintain the records of the board, account for all fees received by the board, supervise and direct employees servicing the board, and perform other services as directed by the board.</p>
          <p>(b) The Department of Administration must provide administrative support in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16B.371">16B.371</a>. The commissioner of administration must assess the board for services it provides under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.07.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">District reimbursement for costs of substitute teachers.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must reimburse local school districts for the costs of substitute teachers employed when regular teachers are providing professional assistance to the state by serving on the board or on a committee or task force appointed by the board and charged to make recommendations concerning standards for teacher licensure in this state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.07.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Public employer compensation reduction prohibited.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The public employer of a member must not reduce the member's compensation or benefits because of the member's absence from employment when engaging in the business of the board.</p>
          <p>(b) The public employer of a member must grant the member time off to join board meetings, committee meetings, and board retreats.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.07.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/25/#laws.0.3.0">Ex1967 c 25 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/749/#laws.0.9.0">1973 c 749 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/136/#laws.0.1.0">1975 c 136 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/271/#laws.0.6.0">1975 c 271 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/149/#laws.0.27.0">1976 c 149 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.22.0">1976 c 222 s 22</a>,23,27,208; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/706/#laws.0.40.0">1978 c 706 s 40</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/793/#laws.0.62.0">1978 c 793 s 62</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/345/#laws.0.9.0">1980 c 345 s 9</a>,10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/251/#laws.0.7.0">1989 c 251 s 7</a>,8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.8.57.0">1998 c 397 art 8 s 57</a>-59,101; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.5.11.0">1998 c 398 art 5 s 11</a>,12; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/96/#laws.2.15.0">2009 c 96 art 2 s 15</a>,16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.12.2.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 12 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/11/#laws.3.1.0">1Sp2019 c 11 art 3 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.12.5.0">2023 c 55 art 12 s 5</a>-10</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.08">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.08 MEETINGS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.08.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Meetings.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must meet regularly at the times and places as the board determines. Meetings must be called by the chair or at the written request of any eight members.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.08.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Executive director.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must have an executive director who is in the unclassified civil service and who is not a member of the board. The executive director must fulfill the duties provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.09#stat.122A.09.6">122A.09, subdivision 6</a>. The board must review the performance of the executive director and set the salary of the executive director, not to exceed the limit for a position listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/15A.0815#stat.15A.0815.2">15A.0815, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.08.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/25/#laws.0.4.0">Ex1967 c 25 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/749/#laws.0.10.0">1973 c 749 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/136/#laws.0.3.0">1975 c 136 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/271/#laws.0.6.0">1975 c 271 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.24.0">1976 c 222 s 24</a>,27; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.8.60.0">1998 c 397 art 8 s 60</a>,101; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.12.3.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 12 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.09">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.09 DUTIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.09.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Code of ethics.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must develop by rule a code of ethics covering standards of professional teaching practices, including areas of ethical conduct and professional performance and methods of enforcement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.09.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Advise members of profession.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must act in an advisory capacity to members of the profession in matters of interpretation of the code of ethics.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.09.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Election of chair and officers.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board shall elect a chair and such other officers as it may deem necessary.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.09.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Licensing and approval.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must license teachers, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.15#stat.122A.15.1">122A.15, subdivision 1</a>, except for supervisory personnel, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.15#stat.122A.15.2">122A.15, subdivision 2</a>. The board must not delegate its authority to make all licensing decisions with respect to applicants for teacher licensure. The board must evaluate applicants for compliance with statutory or rule requirements for licensure and develop licensure verification requirements.</p>
          <p>(b) The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must approve teacher preparation providers seeking to prepare applicants for teacher licensure in Minnesota.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.09.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Reports.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must provide reports in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.091">122A.091</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.09.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.12.23.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 12 s 23</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.09.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Register of persons licensed.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must keep a record of the proceedings of and a register of all persons licensed pursuant to the provisions of this chapter. The register must show the name, licenses and permissions held, including renewals, and license number. This list must be available on the board's website.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.09.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board money.</span></h2>
          <p> All money received by the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board shall be paid into the state treasury as provided by law. The expenses of administering sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120B.363">120B.363</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.01">122A.01</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.15">122A.15</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.16">122A.16</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.26">122A.26</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.40">122A.40</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.41">122A.41</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.42">122A.42</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.45">122A.45</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.49">122A.49</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.54">122A.54</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.55">122A.55</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.56">122A.56</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.57">122A.57</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.58">122A.58</a> that are incurred by the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board shall be paid for from appropriations made to the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.09.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.12.23.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 12 s 23</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.09.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must adopt rules.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must adopt rules subject to the provisions of chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14">14</a> to implement sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120B.363">120B.363</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.05">122A.05 </a>to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.09">122A.09</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.092">122A.092</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.16">122A.16</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.17">122A.17</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.18">122A.18</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.181">122A.181</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.182">122A.182</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.183">122A.183</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.184">122A.184</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.185">122A.185</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.187">122A.187</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.188">122A.188</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.19">122A.19</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.20">122A.20</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.21">122A.21</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.23">122A.23</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.26">122A.26</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.28">122A.28</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.29">122A.29</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.72">124D.72</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) The board must adopt rules relating to fields of licensure and grade levels that a licensed teacher may teach, including a process for granting permission to a licensed teacher to teach in a field that is different from the teacher's field of licensure without change to the teacher's license tier level.</p>
          <p>(c) If a rule adopted by the board is in conflict with a session law or statute, the law or statute prevails. Terms adopted in rule must be clearly defined and must not be construed to conflict with terms adopted in statute or session law.</p>
          <p>(d) The board must include a description of a proposed rule's probable effect on teacher supply and demand in the board's statement of need and reasonableness under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.131">14.131</a>.</p>
          <p>(e) The board must adopt rules only under the specific statutory authority.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.09.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Permissions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding subdivision 9 and sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.055">14.055</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.056">14.056</a>, the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board may grant waivers to its rules upon application by a school district or a charter school for purposes of implementing experimental programs in learning or management.</p>
          <p>(b) To enable a school district or a charter school to meet the needs of students enrolled in an alternative education program and to enable licensed teachers instructing those students to satisfy content area licensure requirements, the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board may permit a licensed teacher teaching in an alternative education program to instruct students in a content area for which the teacher is not licensed, consistent with paragraph (a).</p>
          <p>(c) A special education license permission issued by the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board for a primary employer's low-incidence region is valid in all low-incidence regions.</p>
          <p>(d) An applicant that has obtained career and technical education certification may apply for a Tier 1 license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.181">122A.181</a>. Consistent with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/136F.361">136F.361</a>, the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must strongly encourage approved teacher preparation programs throughout Minnesota to develop alternative pathways for certifying and licensing high school career and technical education instructors and teachers, allowing such applicants to meet certification and licensure standards that demonstrate their content knowledge, classroom experience, and pedagogical practices and their qualifications based on a combination of occupational testing, professional certification or licensure, and long-standing work experience.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.09.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.12.23.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 12 s 23</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
