      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.181">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.181 TIER 1 LICENSE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.181.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Application requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must approve an application for a Tier 1 license in a specified content area if:</p>
          <p>(1) the application has been submitted jointly by the applicant and the district;</p>
          <p>(2) the application has been paid for by the district or the applicant;</p>
          <p>(3) the applicant meets the professional requirement in subdivision 2;</p>
          <p>(4) the district or charter school affirms that the applicant has the necessary skills and knowledge to teach in the specified content area;</p>
          <p>(5) the district or charter school demonstrates that a criminal background check under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.18#stat.122A.18.8">122A.18, subdivision 8</a>, has been completed on the applicant; and</p>
          <p>(6) the district or charter school has posted the teacher position but was unable to hire an acceptable teacher with a Tier 2, 3, or 4 license for the position.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.181.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Special education requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must approve an application for a Tier 1 license in a special education field if:</p>
          <p>(1) the application meets all the requirements under subdivision 1;</p>
          <p>(2) the district or charter school affirms that the applicant will receive high-quality professional development that is sustained, intensive, and classroom focused in order to have a positive and lasting impact on classroom instruction, before and while teaching;</p>
          <p>(3) the district or charter school affirms that the applicant will participate in a program of intensive supervision that consists of structured guidance and regular ongoing support for teachers or a teacher mentoring program; and</p>
          <p>(4) the applicant demonstrates satisfactory progress toward professional licensure.</p>
          <p>(b) A teacher with a Tier 1 license in a special education field may assume the functions as a teacher for a period of time not to exceed three years.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.181.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Professional requirements.</span></h2>
          <p> An applicant for a Tier 1 license must have a bachelor's degree, unless the applicant meets an exemption identified in subdivision 2a.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.181.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Exemptions from a bachelor's degree.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The following applicants for a Tier 1 license are exempt from the requirement to hold a bachelor's degree in subdivision 2:</p>
          <p>(1) an applicant for a Tier 1 license to teach career and technical education or career pathways courses of study if the applicant has:</p>
          <p>(i) an associate's degree;</p>
          <p>(ii) a professional certification; or</p>
          <p>(iii) five years of relevant work experience;</p>
          <p>(2) an applicant for a Tier 1 license to teach world languages and culture pursuant to Minnesota Rules, part <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4950">8710.4950</a>, if the applicant is a native speaker of the language; and</p>
          <p>(3) an applicant for a Tier 1 license in the performing or visual arts pursuant to Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4300">8710.4300</a>, dance and theatre; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4310">8710.4310</a>, dance; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4320">8710.4320</a>, theatre; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4650">8710.4650</a>, vocal music and instrumental music; and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4900">8710.4900</a>, visual arts, if the applicant has at least five years of relevant work experience.</p>
          <p>(b) The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must adopt rules regarding the qualifications and determinations for applicants exempt from paragraph (a).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.181.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Term of license and renewal.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must issue an initial Tier 1 license for a term of one year. A Tier 1 license may be renewed subject to paragraphs (b) and (c).</p>
          <p>(b) The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must renew a Tier 1 license if:</p>
          <p>(1) the district or charter school requesting the renewal demonstrates that it has posted the teacher position but was unable to hire an acceptable teacher with a Tier 2, 3, or 4 license for the position;</p>
          <p>(2) the teacher holding the Tier 1 license took a content examination in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.185">122A.185</a> and submitted the examination results to the teacher's employing district or charter school within one year of the board approving the request for the initial Tier 1 license;</p>
          <p>(3) the teacher holding the Tier 1 license participated in cultural competency training consistent with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120B.30#stat.120B.30.8">120B.30, subdivision 8</a>, within one year of the board approving the request for the initial Tier 1 license; and</p>
          <p>(4) the teacher holding the Tier 1 license met the mental illness training renewal requirement under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.187#stat.122A.187.6">122A.187, subdivision 6</a>.</p>
          <p class="bl">The requirement in clause (2) does not apply to a teacher that teaches a class in a career and technical education or career pathways course of study.</p>
          <p>(c) A Tier 1 license must not be renewed more than three times, unless the requesting district or charter school can show good cause for additional renewals. A Tier 1 license issued to teach (1) a class or course in a career and technical education or career pathway course of study, or (2) in a shortage area, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.06#stat.122A.06.6">122A.06, subdivision 6</a>, may be renewed without limitation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.181.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must accept and review applications for a Tier 1 teaching license beginning July 1 of the school year for which the license is requested; at the board's discretion, the board may begin to accept and review applications before July 1.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.181.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Limitations on license.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A Tier 1 license is limited to the content matter indicated on the application for the initial Tier 1 license under subdivision 1, clause (4), and limited to the district or charter school that requested the initial Tier 1 license.</p>
          <p>(b) A Tier 1 license does not bring an individual within the definition of a teacher for purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.40#stat.122A.40.1">122A.40, subdivision 1</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.41#stat.122A.41.1">122A.41, subdivision 1</a>, clause (a).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.181.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Mentorship and evaluation.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A teacher holding a Tier 1 license must participate in the employing district or charter school's mentorship program and professional development.</p>
          <p>(b) A teacher holding a Tier 1 license must participate in an evaluation aligned, to the extent practicable, with the evaluation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.40#stat.122A.40.8">122A.40, subdivision 8</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.41">122A.41</a>, subdivision 5.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.181.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.3.10.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 3 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/8/#laws.2.2.0">1Sp2020 c 8 art 2 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/53/#laws.11.4.0">2023 c 53 art 11 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.2.65.0">2023 c 55 art 2 s 65; art 5 s 25</a>-30; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/109/#laws.3.5.0">2024 c 109 art 3 s 5</a>,6</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.182">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.182 TIER 2 LICENSE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.182.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Requirements.</span></h2>
          <p> The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must approve an application for a Tier 2 license in a specified content area if:</p>
          <p>(1) the application has been submitted jointly by the applicant and the district;</p>
          <p>(2) the application has been paid for by the district or the applicant;</p>
          <p>(3) the applicant holds a bachelor's degree, unless specifically exempt by statute or rule;</p>
          <p>(4) the district demonstrates that a criminal background check under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.18">122A.18</a>, subdivision 8, has been completed for the applicant; and</p>
          <p>(5) the applicant:</p>
          <p>(i) has completed a state-approved teacher preparation program;</p>
          <p>(ii) is enrolled in a Minnesota-approved teacher preparation program; or</p>
          <p>(iii) has a master's degree in the specified content area.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.182.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Special education requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must approve an application for a Tier 2 license in a special education field if:</p>
          <p>(1) the application meets all the requirements under subdivision 1;</p>
          <p>(2) the district or charter school affirms that the applicant will receive high-quality professional development that is sustained, intensive, and classroom focused in order to have a positive and lasting impact on classroom instruction, before and while teaching;</p>
          <p>(3) the district or charter school affirms that the applicant will participate in a program of intensive supervision that consists of structured guidance and regular ongoing support for teachers or a teacher mentoring program; and</p>
          <p>(4) the applicant demonstrates satisfactory progress toward professional licensure.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.182.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, 2023 c 55 art 5 s 67]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.182.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Exemptions from a bachelor's degree.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The following applicants for a Tier 2 license are exempt from the requirement to hold a bachelor's degree in subdivision 1:</p>
          <p>(1) an applicant for a Tier 2 license to teach career and technical education or career pathways courses of study when the applicant has:</p>
          <p>(i) an associate's degree;</p>
          <p>(ii) a professional certification; or</p>
          <p>(iii) five years of relevant work experience;</p>
          <p>(2) an applicant for a Tier 2 license to teach world languages and culture pursuant to Minnesota Rules, part <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4950">8710.4950</a>, when the applicant is a native speaker of the language; and</p>
          <p>(3) an applicant for a Tier 2 license in the performing or visual arts pursuant to Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4300">8710.4300</a>, dance and theatre; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4310">8710.4310</a>, dance; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4320">8710.4320</a>, theatre; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4650">8710.4650</a>, vocal music and instrumental music; and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4900">8710.4900</a>, visual arts, when the applicant has at least five years of relevant work experience.</p>
          <p>(b) The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must adopt rules regarding the qualifications and determinations for applicants exempt from the requirement to hold a bachelor's degree in subdivision 1.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.182.2b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2b.<span class="headnote">Temporary eligibility for renewal.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) From July 1, 2023, until June 30, 2024, the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must approve an application for a Tier 2 license that meets the coursework requirement under subdivision 2, and other requirements under subdivision 1, clauses (1), (2), (3), and (4).</p>
          <p>(b) For the 2023-2024, 2024-2025, and 2025-2026 school years only, the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must approve an application to renew a Tier 2 license for an applicant that met the Tier 2 requirements in effect at the time the first Tier 2 license was issued. Nothing in this subdivision modifies the renewal requirements in subdivision 3.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.182.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Term of license and renewal.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must issue an initial Tier 2 license for a term of two years. A Tier 2 license may be renewed three times. Before a Tier 2 license is renewed for the first time, a teacher holding a Tier 2 license must participate in cultural competency training consistent with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120B.30#stat.120B.30.8">120B.30, subdivision 8</a>, and mental illness training under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.187#stat.122A.187.6">122A.187, subdivision 6</a>. The board must issue rules setting forth the conditions for additional renewals after the initial license has been renewed three times.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.182.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must accept applications for a Tier 2 teaching license beginning July 1 of the school year for which the license is requested. At the board's discretion, the board may begin to accept and review applications before July 1.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.182.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Limitations on license.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A Tier 2 license is limited to the content matter indicated on the application for the initial Tier 2 license under subdivision 1, and limited to the district or charter school that requested the initial Tier 2 license.</p>
          <p>(b) A Tier 2 license shall not be construed to bring an individual within the definition of a teacher for purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.40#stat.122A.40.1">122A.40, subdivision 1</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.41#stat.122A.41.1">122A.41, subdivision 1</a>, clause (a).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.182.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Application toward probationary period.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The time that a teacher works under a Tier 2 license must be credited towards the teacher's three-year probationary period under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.40#stat.122A.40.5">122A.40, subdivision 5</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.41#stat.122A.41.2">122A.41, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) The time credited towards the probationary period under paragraph (a) must not exceed two years.</p>
          <p>(c) The three years of the probationary period, including any time credited under this subdivision, must run consecutively, consistent with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.40#stat.122A.40.5">122A.40, subdivision 5</a>, or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.41#stat.122A.41.2">122A.41, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.182.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Mentorship and evaluation.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A teacher holding a Tier 2 license must participate in the employing district or charter school's mentorship and evaluation program, including an individual growth and development plan that includes cultural competency under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120B.30#stat.120B.30.8">120B.30, subdivision 8</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) A teacher holding a Tier 2 license must participate in an evaluation aligned, to the extent practicable, with the evaluation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.40#stat.122A.40.8">122A.40, subdivision 8</a>, or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.41#stat.122A.41.5">122A.41, subdivision 5</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.182.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.3.11.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 3 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/8/#laws.2.3.0">1Sp2020 c 8 art 2 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.2.65.0">2023 c 55 art 2 s 65</a>; art 5 s 31-34; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/85/#laws.0.19.0">2024 c 85 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/109/#laws.3.7.0">2024 c 109 art 3 s 7</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.183">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.183 TIER 3 LICENSE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.183.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must issue a Tier 3 license to an applicant who provides information sufficient to demonstrate all of the following:</p>
          <p>(1) the applicant has obtained a passing score on the required licensure exams under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.185">122A.185</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) the applicant holds a bachelor's degree, unless specifically exempt by statute or rule; and</p>
          <p>(3) the applicant has completed the coursework required under subdivision 2.</p>
          <p>(b) The board must issue a Tier 3 license to an applicant who:</p>
          <p>(1) has completed student teaching comparable to the student teaching expectations in Minnesota;</p>
          <p>(2) has obtained a passing score on the required licensure exams under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.185">122A.185</a>; and</p>
          <p>(3) has completed either:</p>
          <p>(i) a teacher preparation program from a culturally specific Minority Serving Institution in the United States, such as Historically Black Colleges and Universities, Tribal Colleges and Universities, or Hispanic-Serving Institutions, including those in Puerto Rico; or</p>
          <p>(ii) a university teacher preparation program in another country.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.183.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Coursework.</span></h2>
          <p>An applicant for a Tier 3 license must meet the coursework requirement by demonstrating one of the following:</p>
          <p>(1) completion of a Minnesota-approved teacher preparation program;</p>
          <p>(2) completion of a state-approved teacher preparation program that includes field-specific student teaching equivalent to field-specific student teaching in Minnesota-approved teacher preparation programs. The field-specific student teaching requirement does not apply to an applicant that has two years of field-specific teaching experience;</p>
          <p>(3) a recommendation for licensure through the licensure via portfolio process;</p>
          <p>(4) a professional teaching license from another state, evidence that the applicant's license is in good standing, and two years of field-specific teaching experience; or</p>
          <p>(5) three years of teaching experience under a Tier 2 license and evidence of summative teacher evaluations that did not result in placing or otherwise keeping the teacher on an improvement process pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.40#stat.122A.40.8">122A.40, subdivision 8</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.41">122A.41</a>, subdivision 5.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.183.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Exemptions from a bachelor's degree.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The following applicants for a Tier 3 license are exempt from the requirement to hold a bachelor's degree in subdivision 1:</p>
          <p>(1) an applicant for a Tier 3 license to teach career and technical education or career pathways courses of study when the applicant has:</p>
          <p>(i) an associate's degree;</p>
          <p>(ii) a professional certification; or</p>
          <p>(iii) five years of relevant work experience;</p>
          <p>(2) an applicant for a Tier 3 license to teach world languages and culture pursuant to Minnesota Rules, part <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4950">8710.4950</a>, when the applicant is a native speaker of the language; and</p>
          <p>(3) an applicant for a Tier 3 license in the performing or visual arts pursuant to Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4300">8710.4300</a>, dance and theatre; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4310">8710.4310</a>, dance; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4320">8710.4320</a>, theatre; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4650">8710.4650</a>, vocal music and instrumental music; and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8710.4900">8710.4900</a>, visual arts, when the applicant has at least five years of relevant work experience.</p>
          <p>(b) The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must adopt rules regarding the qualifications and determinations for applicants exempt from the requirement to hold a bachelor's degree in subdivision 1.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.183.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Term of license and renewal.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must issue an initial Tier 3 license for a term of three years. A Tier 3 license may be renewed every three years without limitation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.183.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Mentorship and evaluation.</span></h2>
          <p>A teacher holding a Tier 3 license must participate in the employing district or charter school's mentorship and evaluation program, including an individual growth and development plan.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.183.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.3.12.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 3 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/182/#laws.1.108.0">2018 c 182 art 1 s 108</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.5.35.0">2023 c 55 art 5 s 35</a>-37; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/109/#laws.3.8.0">2024 c 109 art 3 s 8</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.184">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.184 TIER 4 LICENSE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.184.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must issue a Tier 4 license to an applicant who provides information sufficient to demonstrate all of the following:</p>
          <p>(1) the applicant meets all requirements for a Tier 3 license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.183">122A.183</a>, and: (i) has completed a teacher preparation program under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.183#stat.122A.183.2">122A.183, subdivision 2</a>, clause (1) or (2); (ii) obtained licensure through the licensure via portfolio process under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.183#stat.122A.183.2">122A.183, subdivision 2</a>, clause (3); or (iii) holds national board certification from the National Board for Professional Teaching Standards;</p>
          <p>(2) the applicant has at least three years of field-specific teaching experience as a teacher of record;</p>
          <p>(3) the applicant has obtained a passing score on all required licensure exams under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.185">122A.185</a>; and</p>
          <p>(4) if the applicant previously held a Tier 3 license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.183">122A.183</a>, the applicant has completed the renewal requirements in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.187">122A.187</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.184.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Term of license and renewal.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must issue an initial Tier 4 license for a term of five years. A Tier 4 license may be renewed every five years without limitation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.184.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Mentorship and evaluation.</span></h2>
          <p>A teacher holding a Tier 4 license must participate in the employing district or charter school's mentorship and evaluation program, including an individual growth and development plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.184.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Five-year license.</span></h2>
          <p>A five-year license issued by the commissioner of education before July 1, 2018, must be treated as a Tier 4 license established under this section and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.18">122A.18</a>. An expired five-year license issued by the commissioner of education before July 1, 2018, fulfills the requirements of subdivision 1 for purposes of future licensure by the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.184.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.3.13.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 3 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.5.38.0">2023 c 55 art 5 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/109/#laws.3.9.0">2024 c 109 art 3 s 9</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.185">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.185 TEACHER LICENSURE ASSESSMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.185.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Tests.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The board must adopt rules requiring applicants for Tier 3 and Tier 4 licenses to pass an examination or performance assessment of general pedagogical knowledge and examinations or assessments of licensure field specific content. An applicant is exempt from the examination requirements if the applicant:</p>
          <p>(1) completed a board-approved teacher preparation program;</p>
          <p>(2) completed licensure via portfolio pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.18#stat.122A.18.10">122A.18, subdivision 10</a>, and the portfolio has been recommended;</p>
          <p>(3) obtained national board certification from the National Board for Professional Teaching Standards; or</p>
          <p>(4) completed a state-approved teacher preparation program in another state and passed licensure examinations in that state, if applicable. The content examination requirement does not apply if no relevant content exam exists.</p>
          <p>(b) All testing centers in the state must provide monthly opportunities for untimed content and pedagogy examinations. These opportunities must be advertised on the test registration website. The board must require the exam vendor to provide other equitable opportunities to pass exams, including: (1) waiving testing fees for test takers who qualify for federal grants; (2) providing free, multiple, full-length practice tests for each exam and free, comprehensive study guides on the test registration website; (3) making content and pedagogy exams available in languages other than English for teachers seeking licensure to teach in language immersion programs; and (4) providing free, detailed exam results analysis by test objective to assist applicants who do not pass an exam in identifying areas for improvement. Any applicant who has not passed a required exam after two attempts must be allowed to retake the exam, including new versions of the exam, without being charged an additional fee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.185.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Passing scores.</span></h2>
          <p>The board must establish passing scores in all examinations required for licensure.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.185.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Testing accommodations.</span></h2>
          <p>The board and the entity administering the content and pedagogy examinations must allow any individual who produces documentation of a disability in the form of an evaluation, 504 plan, or individual education program (IEP) to receive the same testing accommodations on the content and pedagogy examinations that the applicant received during the applicant's secondary or postsecondary education.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.185.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>MS 2023 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/109/#laws.3.20.0">2024 c 109 art 3 s 20</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.185.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.3.14.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 3 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.5.39.0">2023 c 55 art 5 s 39</a>,40; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/109/#laws.3.10.0">2024 c 109 art 3 s 10</a>,11</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.187">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.187 EXPIRATION AND RENEWAL.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.187.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">License form requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>Each license issued under this chapter must bear the date of issue and the name of the state-approved teacher training provider or alternative teaching program, as applicable. Licenses must expire and be renewed according to rules adopted by the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board or the Board of School Administrators. The Board of School Administrators shall establish requirements for renewing the licenses of supervisory personnel except athletic coaches.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.187.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Local committees.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must receive recommendations from local committees as established by the board for the renewal of teaching licenses.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.187.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Professional growth.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Applicants for license renewal for a Tier 3 or Tier 4 license under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.183">122A.183</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.184">122A.184</a>, respectively, who have been employed as a teacher during the renewal period of the expiring license, as a condition of license renewal, must present to their local continuing education and relicensure committee or other local relicensure committee evidence of work that demonstrates professional reflection and growth in best teaching practices, including among other things, cultural competence in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120B.30#stat.120B.30.8">120B.30, subdivision 8</a>, and practices in meeting the varied needs of English learners, from young children to adults under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.59">124D.59, subdivisions </a>2 and 2a. A teacher may satisfy the requirements of this paragraph by submitting the teacher's most recent summative evaluation or improvement plan under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.40">122A.40, subdivision </a>8, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.41#stat.122A.41.5">122A.41, subdivision 5</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must ensure that its teacher relicensing requirements include paragraph (a).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.187.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Behavior interventions.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must adopt rules that require all licensed teachers who are renewing a Tier 3 or Tier 4 teaching license under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.183">122A.183</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.184">122A.184</a>, respectively, to include in the renewal requirements further preparation in the areas of using positive behavior interventions and in accommodating, modifying, and adapting curricula, materials, and strategies to appropriately meet the needs of individual students and ensure adequate progress toward the state's graduation rule.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.187.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Reading preparation.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must adopt rules that require all licensed teachers who are renewing a Tier 3 or Tier 4 teaching license under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.183">122A.183</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.184">122A.184</a>, respectively, to include in the renewal requirements further reading preparation, consistent with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120B.118">120B.118</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/120B.124">120B.124</a>. The rules do not take effect until they are approved by law. Teachers who do not provide direct instruction including, at least, counselors, school psychologists, school nurses, school social workers, audiovisual directors and coordinators, and recreation personnel are exempt from this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.187.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Mental illness.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must adopt rules that require all licensed teachers renewing a teaching license under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.181">122A.181</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.184">122A.184</a> to include in the renewal requirements at least one hour of suicide prevention best practices training in each licensure renewal period based on nationally recognized evidence-based programs and practices, among the continuing education credits required to renew a license under this subdivision. Initial training must include understanding the key warning signs of early-onset mental illness in children and adolescents, and during subsequent licensure renewal periods, training must include a more in-depth understanding of students' mental illness trauma, accommodations for students' mental illness, parents' roles in addressing students' mental illness, Fetal Alcohol Spectrum Disorders, autism, the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/125A.0942">125A.0942</a> governing restrictive procedures, and de-escalation methods, among other similar topics.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.187.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">American Indian history and culture.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must adopt rules that require all licensed teachers renewing their license under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.181">122A.181</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.184">122A.184</a> to include in the renewal requirements professional development in the cultural heritage and contemporary contributions of American Indians, with particular emphasis on Minnesota Tribal Nations.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.187.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.3.15.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 3 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/1/8/#laws.2.4.0">1Sp2020 c 8 art 2 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.2.65.0">2023 c 55 art 2 s 65</a>; art 3 s 8; art 5 s 41,42; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/109/#laws.4.20.0">2024 c 109 art 4 s 20</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.188">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.188 LICENSURE DENIAL; APPEAL.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.188.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Denial letter.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must inform an applicant within 30 days of receiving a completed application whether the applicant's application for an initial teaching license or renewal of license has been approved or denied. A completed application must include all supporting information and the results of the background check or conduct determination by the board. When an application is denied, the notification letter must inform the applicant of the process for seeking review of the denial and of the appeals process provided in this section, including all deadlines for seeking review of the denial decision and filing an appeal. The notification letter must identify each licensure requirement the applicant failed to meet.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of this section, "denial" means denial of an initial license or a denial of a renewal license. Denial of an initial license includes a grant of a license that is a lower tier than the applicant applied for and denial of application for an additional field of licensure.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.188.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Review of denial.</span></h2>
          <p>An applicant whose license application is denied may seek review of the denial by submitting a letter to the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board within 30 calendar days of receipt of the denial letter. The applicant may include any documentation necessary to demonstrate that the applicant meets the licensure requirements. The board must review the denial within 60 calendar days of receipt of the letter seeking review. If the board affirms the denial, the board must send the applicant a letter identifying each licensure requirement the applicant failed to meet and informing the applicant of the appeal process provided under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.188.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Appeal.</span></h2>
          <p>An applicant whose application for license or license renewal has been denied under subdivisions 1 and 2 may appeal the decision by filing a written request with the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board within 30 days of notice that the board has affirmed the denial of license. The board must then initiate a contested case under the Administrative Procedure Act, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.001">14.001</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.69">14.69</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.188.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.3.16.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 3 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.5.66.0">2023 c 55 art 5 s 66</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.19">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.19 BILINGUAL AND ENGLISH AS A SECOND LANGUAGE TEACHERS; LICENSES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.19.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Bilingual and English as a second language licenses.</span></h2>
          <p>The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board, hereinafter the board, must grant teaching licenses in bilingual education and English as a second language to persons who present satisfactory evidence that they:</p>
          <p>(1) possess competence and communicative skills in English and in another language;</p>
          <p>(2) possess a bachelor's degree or other academic degree approved by the board, and meet such requirements as to course of study and training as the board may prescribe, consistent with subdivision 4; and</p>
          <p>(3) meet all other requirements for a teaching license provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.18">122A.18</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.184">122A.184</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.19.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Persons holding teaching licenses.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may license a person who holds a teaching license in any tier under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.181">122A.181</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.184">122A.184</a>, respectively, and who presents the board with satisfactory evidence of competence and communicative skills in a language other than English under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.19.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/272/#laws.1.18.0">2014 c 272 art 1 s 18,43</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.19.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Teacher preparation programs.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For the purpose of licensing bilingual and English as a second language teachers, the board may approve teacher preparation programs designed for their training.</p>
          <p>(b) Programs that prepare English as a second language teachers must provide instruction in implementing research-based practices designed specifically for English learners. The programs must focus on developing English learners' academic language proficiency in English, including oral academic language, giving English learners meaningful access to the full school curriculum, developing culturally relevant teaching practices appropriate for immigrant students, and providing more intensive instruction and resources to English learners with lower levels of academic English proficiency and varied needs, consistent with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.59">124D.59, subdivisions 2</a> and 2a.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.19.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Persons eligible for employment.</span></h2>
          <p>Any person licensed under this section is eligible for employment by a school board as a teacher in a bilingual education or English as a second language program in which the language for which the person is licensed is taught or used as a medium of instruction. A board may prescribe only those additional qualifications for teachers licensed under this section that are approved by the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.19.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Affirmative efforts in hiring.</span></h2>
          <p>In hiring for all bilingual education program positions, districts must give preference to and make affirmative efforts to seek, recruit, and employ persons who (1) are native speakers of the language which is the medium of instruction in the bilingual education program or share a native language with the majority of their students, and (2) share the culture of the English learners enrolled in the program. The district shall provide procedures for involving the parent advisory committees in designing the procedures for recruiting, screening, and selecting applicants. This section must not be construed to limit the school board's authority to hire and discharge personnel.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.19.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/306/#laws.0.6.0">1977 c 306 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/609/#laws.3.4.0">1980 c 609 art 3 s 4</a>-7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.8.92.0">1998 c 397 art 8 s 92</a>-94,101; art 11 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/241/#laws.9.9.0">1999 c 241 art 9 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/239/#laws.1.33.0">2012 c 239 art 1 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/272/#laws.1.18.0">2014 c 272 art 1 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.3.17.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 3 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.5.43.0">2023 c 55 art 5 s 43</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.20">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.20 SUSPENSION OR REVOCATION OF LICENSES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.20.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Grounds for revocation, suspension, or denial.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board or Board of School Administrators, whichever has jurisdiction over a teacher's licensure, may, on the written complaint of the school board employing a teacher, a teacher organization, or any other interested person, refuse to issue, refuse to renew, suspend, or revoke a teacher's license to teach for any of the following causes:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) immoral character or conduct;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) failure, without justifiable cause, to teach for the term of the teacher's contract;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) gross inefficiency or willful neglect of duty;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) failure to meet licensure requirements; or</p>
          <p class="in">(5) fraud or misrepresentation in obtaining a license.</p>
          <p class="in">The written complaint must specify the nature and character of the charges.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board or Board of School Administrators, whichever has jurisdiction over a teacher's licensure, shall refuse to issue, refuse to renew, or automatically revoke a teacher's license to teach without the right to a hearing upon receiving a certified copy of a conviction showing that the teacher has been convicted of child abuse, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.185">609.185</a>, sex trafficking in the first degree under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.322#stat.609.322.1">609.322, subdivision 1</a>, sex trafficking in the second degree under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.322">609.322</a>, subdivision 1a, engaging in hiring, or agreeing to hire a minor to engage in prostitution under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.324#stat.609.324.1">609.324, subdivision 1</a>, sexual abuse under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.342">609.342</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.343">609.343</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.344">609.344</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.345">609.345</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.3451#stat.609.3451.3">609.3451, subdivision 3</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.23#stat.617.23.3">617.23, subdivision 3</a>, solicitation of children to engage in sexual conduct or communication of sexually explicit materials to children under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.352">609.352</a>, interference with privacy under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.746">609.746</a> or harassment or stalking under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/609.749">609.749</a> and the victim was a minor, using minors in a sexual performance under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.246">617.246</a>, possessing pornographic works involving a minor under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/617.247">617.247</a>, or any other offense not listed in this paragraph that requires the person to register as a predatory offender under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/243.166">243.166</a>, or a crime under a similar law of another state or the United States. The board shall send notice of this licensing action to the district in which the teacher is currently employed.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) A person whose license to teach has been revoked, not issued, or not renewed under paragraph (b), may petition the board to reconsider the licensing action if the person's conviction for child abuse or sexual abuse is reversed by a final decision of the court of appeals or the supreme court or if the person has received a pardon for the offense. The petitioner shall attach a certified copy of the appellate court's final decision or the pardon to the petition. Upon receiving the petition and its attachment, the board shall schedule and hold a disciplinary hearing on the matter under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/214.10#stat.214.10.2">214.10, subdivision 2</a>, unless the petitioner waives the right to a hearing. If the board finds that, notwithstanding the reversal of the petitioner's criminal conviction or the issuance of a pardon, the petitioner is disqualified from teaching under paragraph (a), clause (1), the board shall affirm its previous licensing action. If the board finds that the petitioner is not disqualified from teaching under paragraph (a), clause (1), it shall reverse its previous licensing action.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) For purposes of this subdivision, the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board is delegated the authority to suspend or revoke coaching licenses.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.20.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Mandatory reporting.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A school board, superintendent, charter school board, charter school executive director, or charter school authorizer must report to the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board, the Board of School Administrators, or the Board of Trustees of the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities, whichever has jurisdiction over the teacher's or administrator's license, when its teacher or administrator is discharged or resigns from employment after a charge is filed with the school board under section 122A.41, subdivisions 6, paragraph (a), clauses (1), (2), and (3), and 7, or after charges are filed that are grounds for discharge under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.40#stat.122A.40.13">122A.40, subdivision 13</a>, paragraph (a), clauses (1) to (5), or when a teacher or administrator is suspended or resigns while an investigation is pending under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.40#stat.122A.40.13">122A.40, subdivision 13</a>, paragraph (a), clauses (1) to (5), or chapter 260E; or 122A.41, subdivisions 6, clauses (1), (2), and (3), and 7; or when a teacher or administrator is suspended without an investigation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.41">122A.41, subdivisions 6, paragraph (a)</a>, clauses (1), (2), and (3), and 7, or chapter 260E. The report must be made to the appropriate licensing board within ten days after the discharge, suspension, or resignation has occurred. The licensing board to which the report is made must investigate the report for violation of subdivision 1 and the reporting board, administrator, or authorizer must cooperate in the investigation. Notwithstanding any provision in chapter 13 or any law to the contrary, upon written request from the licensing board having jurisdiction over the license, a board, charter school, authorizer, charter school executive director, or school superintendent shall provide the licensing board with information about the teacher or administrator from the district's files, any termination or disciplinary proceeding, any settlement or compromise, or any investigative file. Upon written request from the appropriate licensing board, a board or school superintendent may, at the discretion of the board or school superintendent, solicit the written consent of a student and the student's parent to provide the licensing board with information that may aid the licensing board in its investigation and license proceedings. The licensing board's request need not identify a student or parent by name. The consent of the student and the student's parent must meet the requirements of chapter 13 and Code of Federal Regulations, title 34, section 99.30. The licensing board may provide a consent form to the district. Any data transmitted to any board under this section is private data under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.02#stat.13.02.12">13.02, subdivision 12</a>, notwithstanding any other classification of the data when it was in the possession of any other agency.</p>
          <p>(b) The licensing board to which a report is made must transmit to the Attorney General's Office any record or data it receives under this subdivision for the sole purpose of having the Attorney General's Office assist that board in its investigation. When the Attorney General's Office has informed an employee of the appropriate licensing board in writing that grounds exist to suspend or revoke a teacher's license to teach, that licensing board must consider suspending or revoking or decline to suspend or revoke the teacher's or administrator's license within 45 days of receiving a stipulation executed by the teacher or administrator under investigation or a recommendation from an administrative law judge that disciplinary action be taken.</p>
          <p>(c) The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board and Board of School Administrators must report to the appropriate law enforcement authorities a revocation, suspension, or agreement involving a loss of license, relating to a teacher or administrator's inappropriate sexual conduct with a minor. For purposes of this section, "law enforcement authority" means a police department, county sheriff, or Tribal police department. A report by the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board to appropriate law enforcement authorities does not diminish, modify, or otherwise affect the responsibilities of a school board or any person mandated to report abuse under chapter 260E.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.20.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Immunity from liability.</span></h2>
          <p>A school board, its members in their official capacity, and employees of the district run by the board are immune from civil or criminal liability for reporting or cooperating as required under subdivision 2, if their actions required under subdivision 2 are done in good faith and with due care.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.20.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Prohibition on teaching assignment.</span></h2>
          <p>A school district or charter school may not place a teacher in a teaching assignment if the teacher has been criminally charged in state or federal court with any of the offenses listed in subdivision 1, paragraph (b), or is charged with any other offense not listed in this section that requires the person to register as a predatory offender under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/243.166">243.166</a>, or a crime under a similar law of another state or the United States.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.20.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/71/#laws.6.9.0">Ex1959 c 71 art 6 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/25/#laws.0.6.0">Ex1967 c 25 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/869/#laws.0.1.0">1969 c 869 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/155/#laws.0.1.0">1971 c 155 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/749/#laws.0.6.0">1973 c 749 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/271/#laws.0.6.0">1975 c 271 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.15.0">1976 c 222 s 15</a>,27,208; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/345/#laws.0.6.0">1980 c 345 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/97/#laws.0.1.0">1989 c 97 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/375/#laws.0.3.0">1990 c 375 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/25/#laws.0.42.0">2023 c 25 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/109/#laws.3.12.0">2024 c 109 art 3 s 12</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.21">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.21 TEACHERS' AND ADMINISTRATORS' LICENSES; FEES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.21.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Licensure applications.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Each applicant submitting an application to the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board to issue, renew, or extend a teaching license, including applications for licensure via portfolio under subdivision 4, must include a processing fee of $57. The processing fee for a teacher's license and for the licenses of supervisory personnel must be paid to the executive secretary of the appropriate board and deposited in the state treasury. The fees as set by the board are nonrefundable for applicants not qualifying for a license. However, the commissioner of management and budget must refund a fee in any case in which the applicant already holds a valid unexpired license. The board may waive or reduce fees for applicants who apply at the same time for more than one license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.21.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>MS 2017 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.3.36.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 3 s 36</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.21.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Never Effective, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/11/#laws.10.9.0">1Sp2019 c 11 art 10 s 9</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.21.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Licensure via portfolio.</span></h2>
          <p>An applicant must pay to the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board a $300 fee for the first portfolio submitted for review and a $200 fee for any portfolio submitted subsequently. The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board executive secretary must deposit the fee in the education licensure portfolio account in the special revenue fund. The fees are nonrefundable for applicants not qualifying for a license. The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board may waive or reduce fees for applicants based on financial need.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.21.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/71/#laws.6.8.0">Ex1959 c 71 art 6 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/217/#laws.0.1.0">1967 c 217 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/363/#laws.2.2.0">2008 c 363 art 2 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/1/3/#laws.2.17.0">1Sp2015 c 3 art 2 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/189/#laws.33.1.0">2016 c 189 art 33 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.11.5.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 11 s 5-7</a>; art 12 s 22; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/11/#laws.3.9.0">1Sp2019 c 11 art 3 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/55/#laws.5.66.0">2023 c 55 art 5 s 66</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.22">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.22 DISTRICT VERIFICATION OF TEACHER LICENSES.</h1>
        <p>No person shall be accounted a qualified teacher until the school district or charter school contracting with the person for teaching services verifies through the Minnesota education licensing system available on the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board website that the person is a qualified teacher, consistent with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.16">122A.16</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.44#stat.122A.44.1">122A.44, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.22.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/71/#laws.6.11.0">Ex1959 c 71 art 6 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/162/#laws.0.32.0">1975 c 162 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.16.0">1976 c 222 s 16</a>,208; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.8.16.0">1998 c 397 art 8 s 16</a>,101; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/9/#laws.10.6.0">1Sp2003 c 9 art 10 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.12.16.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 12 s 16</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.23">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.23 APPLICANTS TRAINED IN OTHER STATES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.23.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>MS 2017 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.3.36.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 3 s 36</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.23.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>MS 2017 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.3.36.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 3 s 36</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.23.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Teacher licensure agreements with adjoining states.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding any other law to the contrary, the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must enter into a National Association of State Directors of Teacher Education and Certification (NASDTEC) interstate agreement and other interstate agreements for teacher licensure to allow fully certified teachers from adjoining states to transfer their certification to Minnesota. The board must enter into these interstate agreements only after determining that the rigor of the teacher licensure or certification requirements in the adjoining state is commensurate with the rigor of Minnesota's teacher licensure requirements. The board may limit an interstate agreement to particular content fields or grade levels based on established priorities or identified shortages. This subdivision does not apply to out-of-state applicants holding only a provisional teaching license.</p>
          <p>(b) The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must work with designated authorities in adjoining states to establish interstate teacher licensure agreements under this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.23.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/71/#laws.6.6.0">Ex1959 c 71 art 6 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/196/#laws.0.1.0">1965 c 196 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/749/#laws.0.4.0">1973 c 749 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/271/#laws.0.6.0">1975 c 271 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/321/#laws.0.2.0">1975 c 321 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/222/#laws.0.13.0">1976 c 222 s 13</a>,27,208; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/345/#laws.0.4.0">1980 c 345 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.8.14.0">1998 c 397 art 8 s 14</a>,101; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/398/#laws.5.55.0">1998 c 398 art 5 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/489/#laws.6.5.0">2000 c 489 art 6 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/130/#laws.0.12.0">2003 c 130 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/5/#laws.0.3.0">2011 c 5 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/122/#laws.0.3.0">2012 c 122 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/116/#laws.3.12.0">2013 c 116 art 3 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/272/#laws.3.12.0">2014 c 272 art 3 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/21/#laws.1.15.0">2015 c 21 art 1 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/1/3/#laws.2.18.0">1Sp2015 c 3 art 2 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2016/0/189/#laws.24.4.0">2016 c 189 art 24 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.3.19.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 3 s 19</a>; art 12 s 22</p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.122A.24"><b>122A.24</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/5/#laws.0.5.0">2011 c 5 s 5</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.122A.245"><b>122A.245</b> MS 2017 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.3.36.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 3 s 36</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.2451">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.2451 ALTERNATIVE TEACHER PREPARATION PROVIDERS AND PROGRAMS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.2451.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Provider" or "unit" means an eligible entity that seeks or has obtained approval for an alternative teacher preparation program consistent with this section.</p>
          <p>(b) "Program" means content provided by a provider that leads toward licensure in a specific content area.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.2451.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Purpose.</span></h2>
          <p>To provide alternative pathways towards Minnesota teacher licensure outside of the traditional means, improve ethnic and cultural diversity in the classroom, and to close the achievement gap, the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must approve qualified teacher preparation providers and programs under this section that are a means to acquire a Tier 2 license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.182">122A.182</a> and prepare for acquiring a Tier 3 license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.183">122A.183</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.2451.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>A school district, charter school, or nonprofit corporation organized under chapter 317A for an education-related purpose is eligible to participate under this section. An eligible entity may apply for provider and program approval simultaneously.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.2451.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Provider approval.</span></h2>
          <p>An eligible entity must be approved as a provider before being approved to provide programs towards licensure. The Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board must approve eligible entities under subdivision 3 that meet the following requirements:</p>
          <p>(1) has evidence and history of fiscal solvency, capacity, and operation;</p>
          <p>(2) has evidence of necessary infrastructure to provide accurate, timely, and secure data for the purposes of admission, candidate monitoring, testing, background checks, and license recommendations;</p>
          <p>(3) has policies and procedures in place ensuring the security of candidate records under the federal Family Educational Rights and Privacy Act; and</p>
          <p>(4) has the instructional capacity or ability to obtain the instructional capacity to provide an adequate instructional phase under subdivision 5.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.2451.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Program approval.</span></h2>
          <p>The board must approve programs offered by approved providers based on nontraditional criteria. An approved program must have the following characteristics:</p>
          <p>(1) an instructional phase that provides intensive preparation and classroom experience that is commensurate with the scope of licensure standards defined under rule, before the teacher candidate assumes classroom responsibilities;</p>
          <p>(2) a research-based and results-oriented approach focused on best teaching practices to increase student proficiency and growth measured against state academic standards;</p>
          <p>(3) a strategy to combine pedagogy and best teaching practices to better inform teacher candidates' classroom instruction;</p>
          <p>(4) provide assessment, supervision, and evaluation of teacher candidates to determine their specific needs throughout the program, and to support efforts to successfully complete the program;</p>
          <p>(5) provide intensive and ongoing professional learning opportunities that accelerate teacher candidates' professional growth, support student learning, and provide a workplace orientation, professional staff development, mentoring and peer review, focused on standards of professional practice and continuous professional growth; and</p>
          <p>(6) a process to review a candidate's final proficiency of required licensure content standards that leads to potential candidate recommendation by the provider to the board for a Tier 3 teaching license under subdivision 8.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.2451.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Nontraditional means; program instructors.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The board must permit alternative teacher preparation providers and teacher candidates to demonstrate pedagogy and content standard proficiency in school-based programs and through other nontraditional means. Nontraditional means may include previous work experiences, teaching experiences, educator evaluations, industry-recognized certifications, and other essentially equivalent demonstrations.</p>
          <p>(b) The board must use nontraditional criteria to determine qualifications of program instructors, including permitting instructors to hold a baccalaureate degree only.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.2451.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Program disapproval, suspension.</span></h2>
          <p>If the board determines that a teacher preparation provider or licensure program fails to meet or is deficient in any of the requirements of subdivision 5, it may suspend or revoke the approval of the provider or program after it notifies the provider of the deficiencies and gives the provider an opportunity to remedy the deficiencies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.2451.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Candidate program completion; teacher licensure.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A candidate that completes an approved program must apply for a license under the tiered licensure system according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.181">122A.181</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.184">122A.184</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) A person who successfully completes another state's alternative teacher preparation licensure program may apply to the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board for a Tier 3 license under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.183">122A.183</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.2451.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/109/#laws.3.20.0">2024 c 109 art 3 s 20</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.122A.2451.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.3.20.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 3 s 20</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.122A.25"><b>122A.25</b> MS 2017 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/5/#laws.3.36.0">1Sp2017 c 5 art 3 s 36</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.122A.26">
        <h1 class="shn">122A.26 COMMUNITY EDUCATION TEACHERS; LICENSURE REQUIREMENTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.26.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Exemption.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding the provisions of any law to the contrary and except as otherwise provided in this section, a person who teaches in a community education program established pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.18">124D.18</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.19">124D.19</a> is exempt from all licensure requirements.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.26.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Exceptions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A person who teaches in a community education program that qualifies for aid pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.52">124D.52</a> shall continue to meet licensure requirements as a teacher. A person who teaches in an early childhood and family education program that is offered through a community education program and that qualifies for community education aid pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/142D.11">142D.11</a> or early childhood and family education aid pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/124D.135">124D.135</a> shall continue to meet licensure requirements as a teacher. A person who teaches in a community education course that is offered for credit for graduation to persons under 18 years of age shall continue to meet licensure requirements as a teacher.</p>
          <p>(b) A person who teaches a driver training course that is offered through a community education program to persons under 18 years of age shall be licensed by the Professional Educator Licensing and Standards Board or be subject to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/171.35">171.35</a>. A license that is required for an instructor in a community education program pursuant to this paragraph shall not be construed to bring an individual within the definition of a teacher for purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.40#stat.122A.40.1">122A.40, subdivision 1</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/122A.41#stat.122A.41.1">122A.41, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (a).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.122A.26.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">English as a second language.</span></h2>
